for sets, we rely heavily on insert() func to insert a key ^Z3pCwgf2

sort() function - used heavily with vectors ^tHymwyNo

func in vectors.. ^2fXoRcw9

- inbuilt find() function takes substring
and returns the starting index ^juCfm6th

- for binary_search on a sorted vector we use

max(a, max(b,c)) can be done as max({a, b, c})

but max_element({a, b, c}) this is not possible
as for it you would need to create a vector separately
